		return {
			updatePickers(theme = storedTheme || 'auto') {
				document.querySelectorAll('starlight-theme-select').forEach((picker) => {
					const select = picker.querySelector('select');
					if (select) select.value = theme;
					/** @type {HTMLTemplateElement | null} */

					const newIcon = tmpl && tmpl.content.querySelector('.' + theme);

						if (oldIcon) {
							oldIcon.replaceChildren(...newIcon.cloneNode(true).childNodes);
						}
					}
				});
			},
		};
	})();

<div class="sl-heading-wrapper level-h2"><h2 id="overview">Overview</h2><a class="sl-anchor-link" href="#overview"><span aria-hidden="true" class="sl-anchor-icon"><svg width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="currentColor"><path d="m12.11 15.39-3.88 3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1-3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0-3.5l3.88-3.88a1 1 0 1 0-1.42-1.42l-3.88 3.89a4.48 4.48 0 0 0 6.33 6.33l3.89-3.88a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42m8.58-12.08a4.49 4.49 0 0 0-6.33 0l-3.89 3.88a1 1 0 1 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1 3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0 3.5l-3.88 3.88a1 1 0 0 0 0 1.42 1 1 0 0 0 1.42 0l3.88-3.89a4.49 4.49 0 0 0 0-6.33M8.83 15.17a1 1 0 0 0 .71.29 1 1 0 0 0 .71-.29l4.92-4.92a1 1 0 1 0-1.42-1.42l-4.92 4.92a1 1 0 0 0 0 1.42"></path></svg></span><span class="sr-only" data-pagefind-ignore>Section titled “Overview”</span></a></div>
<p>The <a href="https://github.com/openai/openai-agents-js">OpenAI Agents SDK for TypeScript</a> enables you to build agentic AI apps in a lightweight, easy-to-use package with very few abstractions. It’s a production-ready upgrade of our previous experimentation for agents, <a href="https://github.com/openai/swarm/tree/main">Swarm</a>, that’s also <a href="https://github.com/openai/openai-agents-python">available in Python</a>. The Agents SDK has a very small set of primitives:</p>
<ul>
<li><strong>Agents</strong>, which are LLMs equipped with instructions and tools</li>
<li><strong>Sandbox agents</strong>, which pair agents with isolated filesystem workspaces, shell commands, file editing, snapshots, and sandbox session state</li>
<li><strong>Voice agents</strong>, which support low-latency spoken interactions with tools, guardrails, handoffs, and conversation history</li>
<li><strong>Agents as tools and handoffs</strong>, which allow agents to delegate to other agents for specific tasks</li>
<li><strong>Guardrails</strong>, which enable the inputs to agents to be validated</li>
</ul>
<p>In combination with TypeScript, these primitives are powerful enough to express complex relationships between tools and agents, give agents a real workspace when they need one, and allow you to build real-world applications without a steep learning curve. In addition, the SDK comes with built-in <strong>tracing</strong> that lets you visualize and debug your agentic flows, as well as evaluate them and even fine-tune models for your application.</p>
<div class="sl-heading-wrapper level-h2"><h2 id="why-use-the-agents-sdk">Why use the Agents SDK</h2><a class="sl-anchor-link" href="#why-use-the-agents-sdk"><span aria-hidden="true" class="sl-anchor-icon"><svg width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="currentColor"><path d="m12.11 15.39-3.88 3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1-3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0-3.5l3.88-3.88a1 1 0 1 0-1.42-1.42l-3.88 3.89a4.48 4.48 0 0 0 6.33 6.33l3.89-3.88a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42m8.58-12.08a4.49 4.49 0 0 0-6.33 0l-3.89 3.88a1 1 0 1 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1 3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0 3.5l-3.88 3.88a1 1 0 0 0 0 1.42 1 1 0 0 0 1.42 0l3.88-3.89a4.49 4.49 0 0 0 0-6.33M8.83 15.17a1 1 0 0 0 .71.29 1 1 0 0 0 .71-.29l4.92-4.92a1 1 0 1 0-1.42-1.42l-4.92 4.92a1 1 0 0 0 0 1.42"></path></svg></span><span class="sr-only" data-pagefind-ignore>Section titled “Why use the Agents SDK”</span></a></div>
<p>The SDK has two driving design principles:</p>
<ol>
<li>Enough features to be worth using, but few enough primitives to make it quick to learn.</li>
<li>Works great out of the box, but you can customize exactly what happens.</li>
</ol>
<p>Here are the main features of the SDK:</p>
<ul>
<li><strong>Agent loop</strong>: A built-in agent loop that handles tool invocation, sends results back to the LLM, and continues until the task is complete.</li>
<li><strong>Sandbox execution</strong>: Run agents with isolated filesystem workspaces, shell commands, file editing, snapshots, and sandbox session state when the work needs a workspace.</li>
<li><strong>Voice Agents</strong>: Build low-latency spoken interactions with features such as automatic interruption detection, context management, guardrails, and more.</li>
<li><strong>TypeScript-first</strong>: Orchestrate and chain agents using native TypeScript language features, without needing to learn new abstractions.</li>
<li><strong>Agents as tools and handoffs</strong>: A powerful mechanism for coordinating and delegating work across multiple agents.</li>
<li><strong>Guardrails</strong>: Run input validation and safety checks in parallel with agent execution, and fail fast when checks do not pass.</li>
<li><strong>Function tools</strong>: Turn any TypeScript function into a tool with automatic schema generation and schema-based validation.</li>
<li><strong>MCP server tool calling</strong>: Built-in integration that exposes tools from MCP servers to agents alongside function tools.</li>
<li><strong>Sessions</strong>: A persistent memory layer for maintaining working context within an agent loop.</li>
<li><strong>Human in the loop</strong>: Built-in mechanisms for involving humans across agent runs.</li>
<li><strong>Tracing</strong>: Built-in tracing for visualizing, debugging, and monitoring workflows, with support for the OpenAI suite of evaluation, fine-tuning, and distillation tools.</li>
</ul>
<div class="sl-heading-wrapper level-h2"><h2 id="installation">Installation</h2><a class="sl-anchor-link" href="#installation"><span aria-hidden="true" class="sl-anchor-icon"><svg width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="currentColor"><path d="m12.11 15.39-3.88 3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1-3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0-3.5l3.88-3.88a1 1 0 1 0-1.42-1.42l-3.88 3.89a4.48 4.48 0 0 0 6.33 6.33l3.89-3.88a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42m8.58-12.08a4.49 4.49 0 0 0-6.33 0l-3.89 3.88a1 1 0 1 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1 3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0 3.5l-3.88 3.88a1 1 0 0 0 0 1.42 1 1 0 0 0 1.42 0l3.88-3.89a4.49 4.49 0 0 0 0-6.33M8.83 15.17a1 1 0 0 0 .71.29 1 1 0 0 0 .71-.29l4.92-4.92a1 1 0 1 0-1.42-1.42l-4.92 4.92a1 1 0 0 0 0 1.42"></path></svg></span><span class="sr-only" data-pagefind-ignore>Section titled “Installation”</span></a></div>
<div class="expressive-code"><link rel="stylesheet" href="/openai-agents-js/_astro/ec.by82b.css"><script type="module" src="/openai-agents-js/_astro/ec.0vx5m.js"></script><figure class="frame is-terminal not-content"><figcaption class="header"><span class="title"></span><span class="sr-only">Terminal window</span></figcaption><pre data-language="bash"><code><div class="ec-line"><div class="code"><span style="--0:#00DAEF;--1:#3360C1">npm</span><span style="--0:#EEF0F9;--1:#383A42"> </span><span style="--0:#FFD493;--1:#387138">install</span><span style="--0:#EEF0F9;--1:#383A42"> </span><span style="--0:#FFD493;--1:#387138">@openai/agents</span><span style="--0:#EEF0F9;--1:#383A42"> </span><span style="--0:#FFD493;--1:#387138">zod</span></div></div></code></pre><div class="copy"><div aria-live="polite"></div><button title="Copy to clipboard" data-copied="Copied!" data-code="npm install @openai/agents zod"><div></div></button></div></figure></div>
<p>The SDK requires Zod v4; installing <code dir="auto">zod</code> via npm will fetch the latest v4 release.</p>
<div class="sl-heading-wrapper level-h2"><h2 id="choose-your-starting-point">Choose your starting point</h2><a class="sl-anchor-link" href="#choose-your-starting-point"><span aria-hidden="true" class="sl-anchor-icon"><svg width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="currentColor"><path d="m12.11 15.39-3.88 3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1-3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0-3.5l3.88-3.88a1 1 0 1 0-1.42-1.42l-3.88 3.89a4.48 4.48 0 0 0 6.33 6.33l3.89-3.88a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42m8.58-12.08a4.49 4.49 0 0 0-6.33 0l-3.89 3.88a1 1 0 1 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1 3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0 3.5l-3.88 3.88a1 1 0 0 0 0 1.42 1 1 0 0 0 1.42 0l3.88-3.89a4.49 4.49 0 0 0 0-6.33M8.83 15.17a1 1 0 0 0 .71.29 1 1 0 0 0 .71-.29l4.92-4.92a1 1 0 1 0-1.42-1.42l-4.92 4.92a1 1 0 0 0 0 1.42"></path></svg></span><span class="sr-only" data-pagefind-ignore>Section titled “Choose your starting point”</span></a></div>
<p>Most first-time users only need one of these entry points:</p>

























<table><thead><tr><th>Start with</th><th>Use it when</th><th>Notes</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code dir="auto">@openai/agents</code></td><td>You are building most text, sandbox, or realtime applications.</td><td>Recommended default. It includes the OpenAI provider setup, sandbox agent APIs under <code dir="auto">@openai/agents/sandbox</code>, and realtime APIs under <code dir="auto">@openai/agents/realtime</code>.</td></tr><tr><td><code dir="auto">@openai/agents-realtime</code></td><td>You only need the standalone Realtime package.</td><td>Useful for browser-only realtime apps or when you want a narrower package boundary.</td></tr><tr><td>Lower-level packages (<code dir="auto">@openai/agents-core</code>, <code dir="auto">@openai/agents-openai</code>, <code dir="auto">@openai/agents-extensions</code>)</td><td>You need lower-level composition, custom provider wiring, or specific integrations.</td><td>Most new users can ignore these until they have a concrete need.</td></tr></tbody></table>
<div class="sl-heading-wrapper level-h2"><h2 id="hello-world-examples">Hello world examples</h2><a class="sl-anchor-link" href="#hello-world-examples"><span aria-hidden="true" class="sl-anchor-icon"><svg width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="currentColor"><path d="m12.11 15.39-3.88 3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1-3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0-3.5l3.88-3.88a1 1 0 1 0-1.42-1.42l-3.88 3.89a4.48 4.48 0 0 0 6.33 6.33l3.89-3.88a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42m8.58-12.08a4.49 4.49 0 0 0-6.33 0l-3.89 3.88a1 1 0 1 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1 3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0 3.5l-3.88 3.88a1 1 0 0 0 0 1.42 1 1 0 0 0 1.42 0l3.88-3.89a4.49 4.49 0 0 0 0-6.33M8.83 15.17a1 1 0 0 0 .71.29 1 1 0 0 0 .71-.29l4.92-4.92a1 1 0 1 0-1.42-1.42l-4.92 4.92a1 1 0 0 0 0 1.42"></path></svg></span><span class="sr-only" data-pagefind-ignore>Section titled “Hello world examples”</span></a></div>
<p>Start with a regular <code dir="auto">Agent</code> for text workflows. Use a sandbox agent when the agent should work in a filesystem or carry workspace state across longer tasks.</p>
